suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha…pai॰… uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ  nuppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

47. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai akusalai cittai vattamānai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

(3) anāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

48. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca  uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰… uppaj‍jissatīti? āmantā|

49. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati  maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

59. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

61. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa uppatticittassa (syā॰) uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha  samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai  arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

abhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

66. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ  upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti?

abhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa uppādakkhaṇai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ  cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

67. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti?

abhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(5) pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

68. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ  uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ  uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

69. (ka) yassa  samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai (sī॰ syā॰) puggalaukāsavāraipi cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya  uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

70. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati…pai॰… (yatthakampi yassayatthakasadisaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

71. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa  ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

72. (ka) yassa  yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai  taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

74. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti?

76. (ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ  tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa  cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñattā cavantānaṃ arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati  tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa  bhaṅgakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

77. (ka) yassa  yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati| aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ, āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ  tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(6) atītānāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

78. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

79. 81. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārānaṃ pañ‍cavaukārānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau  ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārānaṃ pañ‍cavaukārānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ  uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| yassa cittassa  anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

82. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha| yassa cittassa  anantarā aggamaggaṃ  paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

83. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti? natthi|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

86. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti? uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

(ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti? uppaj‍jissati|

87. (ka) yassa  yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissatīti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jitthāti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jittha| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jittha|

uppādavārau|

2. pavatti 2. niraudhavārau

(1) pac‍cuppan‍navārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

88. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai taṇhāvippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ  nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| pañ‍cavaukārai maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

arūpai maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau  ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| pañ‍cavaukārai maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

89. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

90. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

asañ‍ñasattai tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(yatthakaṃ uppādaipi niraudhaipi uppādaniraudhaipi sadisaṃ, natthi nānākaraṇaṃ)|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

91. yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(yassayatthakampi sadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

92. (ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai taṇhāvippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

arūpai  maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai phalassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai  arūpai phalassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

93. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttamaggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ  taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai maggavippayuttataṇhāvippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

94. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

95. yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(yassakampi yassakampi yatthakampi (sī॰ syā॰) yassayatthakampi sadisaṃ, yassayatthakaipi niraudhasamāpan‍nānanti caitaṃ na kātabbaṃ)|

(2) atītavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

96. yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti? āmantā|

(yathā uppādavārai atītā pucchā anulaumampi pac‍canīkampi vibhattā aivaṃ niraudhaipi vibhajitabbā, natthi nānākaraṇaṃ)|

(3) anāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

97. (ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai (sī॰ syā॰) uppādavārai pana pāṭhantaraṃ natthi taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…| āmantā|

98. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ  nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

99. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati…pai॰…|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

100. yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārānaṃ pañ‍cavaukārānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati…pai॰…|

(yassakampi yassayatthakampi sadisaṃ)|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

101. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa  anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| pacchimacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| pacchimacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

102. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? āmantā|

105. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

106. (ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

sabbaisaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti?

anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

abhisamaitāvīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai  cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

107. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti?

anabhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha|

(kha) yassa  vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

abhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| abhisamaitāvīnaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

116. (ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

117. (ka) yassa  samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ  nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

118. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

119. (ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha  samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

120. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhati|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

121. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai  maggassa  ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca  na nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

122. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya  uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa  samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai vattamānai, niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

124. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

129. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārānaṃ pañ‍cavaukārānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha| itaraisaṃ catuvaukārānaṃ pañ‍cavaukārānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha|

130. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti|

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhittha| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhittha|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

131. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? natthi|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti? nirujjhittha|

(ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? natthi|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti? nirujjhittha|

132. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? natthi|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti? nirujjhittha|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

133. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha…pai॰…|

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

134. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti? nirujjhittha|

(ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti? nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti? nirujjhittha|

135. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ dutiyai cittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhittha maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhitthāti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti āpāyikānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ  na nirujjhittha| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhittha|

niraudhavārau|

2. pavatti 3. uppādaniraudhavārau

(1) pac‍cuppan‍navārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

136. (ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti? nau|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti? nau|

137. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti? nau|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

138. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti?

asañ‍ñasattai tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhati…pai॰…|

(yatthakaṃ uppādavāraipi niraudhavāraipi uppādaniraudhavāraipi sadisaṃ natthi nānākaraṇaṃ)|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

139. yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhatīti? nau|

(yassakampi yassayatthakampi sadisaṃ)|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

140. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai taṇhāvippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca  na nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai taṇhāvippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

141. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhatīti?

maggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati| taṇhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati|

(kha) yassa vā  pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| maggavippayuttacittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

145. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgīnaṃ arahantānaṃ yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

(ka) yassa  dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

146. (ka) yassa  samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

150. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca  nuppaj‍jissati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(4) pac‍cuppan‍nātītavārau

153. yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhitthāti? āmantā|

(pac‍cuppan‍naatītā pac‍cuppan‍nainātītā (syā॰) pucchā uppādavāraipi uppādaniraudhavāraipi yassakampi yatthakampi yassayatthakampi anulaumampi pac‍canīkampi sadisaṃ, asammauhantaina vibhajitabbā)|

(5) pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

154. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ  nirujjhissati| itaraisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa  ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

155. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai  maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

156. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati…pai॰…|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

157. (ka) yassa  yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ  nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ…pai॰… itaraisaṃ catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ…pai॰…|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

158. (ka) yassa yattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca  taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti  yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

159. (ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca  nuppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa  bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ  cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggaphalassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggaphalassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

160. (ka) yassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ  paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

yai puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ  maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai taisaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

161. yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati…pai॰…|

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

162. (ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa  bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa  anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggamaggassa  ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tassa cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai maggassa ca phalassa ca uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggaphalassa  uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā apāyā asañ‍ñasattā (syā॰) cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati tassa tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

arahantānaṃ cittassa uppādakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ apāyaṃ asañ‍ñasattaṃ (syā॰) upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha dukkhasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arūpai aggaphalassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

163. (ka) yassa  yattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati tassa tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissatīti?

aggamaggassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā aggamaggaṃ paṭilabhissanti yai cañ‍ñai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai  vattamānai taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha maggasac‍caṃ  na nirujjhissati tassa tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jatīti?

āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍caṃ na nirujjhissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha samudayasac‍caṃ nuppaj‍jati| aggamaggassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai arahantānaṃ āpāyikānaṃ yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti taisaṃ taṇhāya bhaṅgakkhaṇai taṇhāvippayuttacittai vattamānai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha maggasac‍cañ‍ca na nirujjhissati samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nuppaj‍jati|

(6) atītānāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

164. yassa dukkhasac‍caṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa samudayasac‍caṃ nirujjhissatīti?

(yathā niraudhavārai atītānāgatā atītainānāgatā (syā॰) pucchā anulaumampi pac‍canīkampi vibhattā aivaṃ uppādaniraudhavāraipi asammauhantaina vibhajitabbaṃ)|

uppādaniraudhavārau|

pavattivārau niṭṭhitau|

3. pariñ‍ñāvārau

1. pac‍cuppan‍navārau

165. (ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ pajahati sau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānātīti? āmantā|

(ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

2. atītavārau

166. (ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānittha sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

(ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānittha sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

3. anāgatavārau

167. (ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānissati sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahissatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

(ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānissati sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissatīti? āmantā|

(kha) yau vā pana…pai॰…? āmantā|

4. pac‍cuppan‍nātītavārau

168. (ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahitthāti? nau|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ pajahittha sau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānātīti? nau|

(ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahitthāti?

arahā dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti, nau ca samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahittha| aggamaggasamaṅgiñ‍ca arahantañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā avasaisā puggalā dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānanti samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahittha|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahittha sau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānātīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgī samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahittha, nau ca dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti| aggamaggasamaṅgiñ‍ca arahantañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā avasaisā puggalā samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahittha dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānanti|

5. pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatavārau

169. (ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahissatīti? nau|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ pajahissati sau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānātīti? nau|

(ka) yau  dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissatīti?

yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tai dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānanti, nau ca samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissanti| arahā yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti  tai dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānanti samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahissanti|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissati sau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānātīti?

aggamaggasamaṅgī samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissati, nau ca dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānāti| arahā yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti tai samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahissanti dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānanti|

6. atītānāgatavārau

170. (ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānittha sau samudayasac‍caṃ pajahissatīti? nau|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ pajahissati sau dukkhasac‍caṃ parijānitthāti? nau|

(ka) yau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānittha sau samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissatīti?

yai maggaṃ paṭilabhissanti tai dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānittha, nau ca tai samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissanti| aggamaggasamaṅgī yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti tai dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānittha samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahissanti|

(kha) yau vā pana samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissati sau dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānitthāti?

arahā samudayasac‍caṃ nappajahissati, nau ca dukkhasac‍caṃ na parijānittha| aggamaggasamaṅgī yai ca puthuj‍janā maggaṃ na paṭilabhissanti tai  samudayasac‍cañ‍ca nappajahissanti dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca na parijānittha|

pariñ‍ñāvārau|

sac‍cayamakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
